# Барааны бүх үзүүлэлт харах — Power Fx formulas

### `Барааны бүх үзүүлэлт харах` . **OnVisible**  _(Screen)_
```powerfx
Set(pgAccess,Blank());If(Connection.Connected,IfError(Refresh(AssetUserAccess);Set(pgAccess,LookUp(AssetUserAccess,CompanyCode="TME-Inventory-Pages" && UserEmail=Lower(User().Email))),Set(pgAccess,Blank())));If((Lower(User().Email)="od.u@tme.mn" || (pgAccess.Active && (IsBlank(pgAccess.ExpiresAt) || pgAccess.ExpiresAt>Now()) && "|stock|" in pgAccess.Title && !IsBlank(pgAccess.LocationCode))),false
)
```

